{"forms":[{"form":"bristles","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"bristling","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"bristled","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"bristled","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"His hair began to bristle with anger when the subject was mentioned.","type":"example"},{"text":"The cat’s fur bristled when the dog approached.","type":"example"},{"text":"The soldier’s body would bristle with tension before a battle.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To rise or stand erect, like bristles."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"glosses":["To abound, to be covered with, or to have an abundance of, something, especially something jutting out."],"raw_tags":["with with"],"tags":["intransitive","usually"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The employees bristled at the prospect of working through the holidays.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To be on one's guard or raise one's defenses; to react with fear, suspicion, or distance."],"raw_tags":["with at or with"],"tags":["intransitive","usually"]},{"glosses":["To make (something) rise or stand erect, like bristles."],"tags":["archaic","obsolete","transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Your blatant attitude always bristles me.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To cause (someone) to be on one's guard or raise one's defenses."],"tags":["transitive","uncommon"]},{"examples":[{"text":"to bristle a thread","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To fix a bristle or bristles to."],"tags":["rare"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈbɹɪsəl/"},{"ipa":"/ˈbɹʌsəl/"},{"ipa":"/ˈbɹɪstəl/"}],"upstream_line":225615,"upstream_offset":1110258768,"upstream_record_sha256":"306a877f1973b9b17ef2d2531783ad895914a86f47b7dc5d9700213139fcad26","word":"bristle"}
